Under the neon glow I see my shadowed self in a trance
I feel the weight of the world press down, like gravity's pull on a broken clock
Through the fog I see the stars, neon lights twist and turn like scars, gravity's grip pulls me down, but my soul soars like
I'm a ghost in the machine, floating in the void of my mind
<END_SONG>

In the dead of night I'm a ghost on the wire
I see my ghostly reflection in the glassy night, my breath a mist in the cold, a ghostly wail as I fight to break free
I'm an atom floating in a sea of neon light I'm a ghost with a thousand faces, each one a different sight I'm a storm in the dark
I'm a shadow of myself in the neon glow, bending time, twisting my soul to a slow
I'm a ghost in the machine, echoes of the dead
With eyes on the floor and thoughts in the sky

I'm a ghost in this hall of mirrors, echoes of my tortured soul
I'm a cipher, a ghost, a shadow, a trail
The cipher whispers in my ear, a hollow, cold impale, I see my soul as clear as steel, the rusted vaults of hell prevail
Corrosion creeps through vents, crypts cryin' for relief
I'm a cipher, I'm a ghost, a specter in the void, a phantom, a soul in chains, I'm the echo, I'm
In the shadows of the night's cold breath, I whisper, "ciphered dreams"
Through the crypts of concrete I hear the whispers, echoes, of forgotten dreams, neon fades into the night, where shadows play hide and seek, I'm
The ciphered whispers creep through the rusted vents, a cold breeze, a silent scream, a broken heart, a forgotten dream
I'm a ghost in a haunted attic, whispers in the night's embrace
I'm a ghost in the shadows, whispers in the pipes
Through the cryptic whispers I hear my shadow scream
In the labyrinth of whispers I'm lost in the maze
Through the pipes I whisper cryptic riddles, echoes in the hollows of my mind
I'm the cipher in the shadows, my mind's a fortress, locked tight
Ciphered whispers in the vent, echoing through the cryptic night

When I hit that dirt I feel the burnin' court
I'm the alchemist fashin' the beast's breath, snail-slow exhale of a thousand lies, be
I'm a ghost in the machine, a cold, calculated soul, a man of steel, not soft, not whole, not fail, I'm impale
In the shadow of the forge I'm tempering my tongue
I'm a shaper of night's shadow, a sculptor of flame
I craft my rhymes with precision, like a sculptor shaping metal, my flow a weapon in the war, I'm the forger of my own destiny
I craft my malice in silence, a scorching chill in the air
I'll tear this up like a grizzled old man with a new fang
I'm sifting through the ash, igniting flames from metal dreams
I'm the shadow in the corner where the light don't reach
My mind's a furnace, burnin' through the blueprint, feelin' like a ghost in a machine, sippin' from the ashes, heat
Glowing neon eyes in the cold concrete vault, echoes of chains rattling in the night
I'm a machine, a fire-breathing dragon of rage
In the shadow of the machine I'm carving my mark,
In the forge I mold the future, fire's my crucible, I'm the sculptor, my hand is relentless

I'm a specter, not a savior, I haunt the shadows, not the sun's caress

Ciphers woven through the circuitry, glitching out my mind, synths cryin' like I'm bleedin', echo-echo, I'm lost
Rusty wires buzz, like a glitch in the zone, sifting through code, where my soul's in a zone
<END_SONG>
I'm a ghost in the machine, my soul's a dusty court
I was a ghost in the attic, the tapes were my secret, the dirt was my hurt, the court was my quart, I whispered to the villain
I'll take the backseat when the lights come down and shine
A lone wolf howls at the moonlight, his voice echoes through the alleys, a ghostly tale, a failed soul's wail, his heart
In the shadows, I'm the voice of the night, my whispers through the speakers ignite
Echoes from the lost archives, ancient whispers of the night
The past's echoes, they haunt me, like a ghost in a mist
In the dim glow of the flickering light
I'm the ghost in the machine, the phantom of the scene
I'm the ghost in the attic, with whispers of the past
A haunted house of echoes I'm locked in a loop of past mistakes
Echoes of the lost, where shadows play the lead
Echoes from yesteryear, when the mixtapes whispered, now I'm the villain, ain't no need for regret, I'm the ghost
Ghosts in the attic hauntin' me, scratchin' at the ceiling,
The tapes, they're lost, in the shadows of my past, my mind's a labyrinth, a maze of pain, I'm the ghost in the machine
Forgotten mixtapes, whispers of the night

I'm under the neon sign, a man with the weight of the world
I'm feelin' weightless, ghostin' 'round the house
I'm in the shadows, where the light's not quite right, I'm a ghost in the hallways, where the echoes take flight, I can't
Underneath the neon's pale, I hold my soul in thrall, like a prisoner in a cell, feeling the chains enthrall, in this gravity
I'm a ghost in the digital void, haunting the screenscraped code, in a world where my soul's weight is measured in bytes, where the gravity
The flicker of neon's pulse syncs my mind to the grind
In the neon's haze, I'm lost in the maze, shadows cast by flicker, weight of unseen masses, gravity-wrapped flows, I'm not
Gravity-warping through the night, neon bulbs flicker in my sight, mind's a warping lens, twisted thoughts in flight, weightless soul
Glitches in my synapses, neon veins of code
In the dim room where shadows dance and ghostly whispers chase
I'm the ghost in the machine, weaving through the void's cold embrace
I'm floating in a haze, stars in my eyes
I'm weightless, like a cloud, in a void of neon light
Gliding through the void with weightless feet, shadows dance beneath the neon street
Through the neon haze, I'm carving through the night, my mind's a churning sea, beneath the flicker of my eyes, I'm weightless
Gotta break through the haze and chase my muse like a madman in a maze

I'm a ghost in the corner with a paint can in hand
I paint my rhymes on the walls, in a way that feels like they're real
I'm on a mission to unveil the unseen, a haze that settles on the page, a krylon mist on half-finished rhyme diagrams, where shadows
Krylon mist settles over half-finished rhyme diagrams
I'm painting a picture with a mind's eye view, shades of grey, hues of blue,
I'm a ghost in the attic, dust settles on my rhymes
I'm a ghost in the alley, my soul's on the run
I'm paintin' walls with ink, where shadows hide and secrets link,
Krylon's spray on the wall, half-erased dreams in a half-drawn veil
Krylon's a haze, like a ghostly shade, my rhymes are half-formed, sketches on a page, sprayin' in the dark
I'm a ghost on the wall, my shadow's a gale,
I'm spittin' krylon mist over unfinished rhyme diagrams
I'm a ghost with a soul on a quest to find a place to call my own
Inhaling krylon's mist, exhaling lines of fire
The air thickens like the fog that swallows up the night,
Krylon mist settles in half-finished rhyme diagrams / Echoes of silence in the drafty attic walls / Ghosts of old verses whisper through the crack

Stacks of old comic books and vinyls worn to dirt
A dusty court, a cracked quart, a world turned hurt
I'm just a ghostly figure in the attic, a dusty relic in this world so bleak
In the dusty corner where the comics laid to waste
Dusty comics framed in time, vinyl ghosts whisper in the dark
The torn pages whisper tales of the past, like broken cassettes playing in the dark
Dusty comics, tales of battles fought, scratched vinyl whispers, memories not bought
Dusty comics piled high, vinyl warped by time's embrace
Dusted comics stacked, tape reels cracked and stale
Dusty frames of lost smiles in the light, scratchy reels of the old machine, I hear the whispers, they're just the echoes of my mind
Dusty comics on the shelf, I'm a ghost of the reel, echoes in the hall, I'm the phantom of the deal, lost in the labyrinth
Dusty frames of comics, tales of heroes, villains, and dreams
Dusty comics, faded frames, tales of heroes, villains, and names
Dusty comics framed in time, vinyl tales in glassine line
Cracked vinyl whispers secrets, ghostly voices in the dark
I'm a ghost in the attic of my mind, with stories in my spine, like faded comics and old reel-to-reel, the echoes of the past

I'm like the puppeteer in the theater, strings too long, gotta cut the thread
With a smirk I eye the shadows creepin' through the streets
I see the future, through the prism of time's decay
As I step through the shadows of the night
In the shadows I'm the silent whisper, stalking through the night's embrace
I'm the shadow in the corner, where the light never goes
I'm the shadow, the specter, the one they fear
Inside the vault, I'm a thief with a grudge, stealin' memories from every mug, breakin' hearts like a blade through a rug
<END_SONG>
